- NOTE 08-28 04:40: **GPU packing for the WM runs.** Batched Genesis rejected (probe 2970418). r2dreamer is CPU-bound: sacct 2970588 AveCPU 39.9 h over 8.5 h elapsed (~4.7 cores), MaxRSS 33 GB, ~15 h wall at 1.75M/3M steps; pax049 (64 cores, 8 GPUs) ran 8 such jobs at load 138 -- 2x thread oversubscription (no OMP/torch thread limits in the launcher). New `cluster/sbatch_r2dreamer_pack.sh`: one job = 1 GPU, 16 CPUs, 96 GB, PACK_SEEDS runs of the UNCHANGED launcher in parallel, each with OMP/MKL threads = 8, own `.out` (`r2d_train_<job>_s<seed>.out`). Pilot: dR2Ddup13 s202+s203 (queued jobs 2981321/22 cancelled, packed job 2981480). Two consequences to disclose: packed runs share a job id in RUN_REGISTRY; thread count differs from the unpacked runs (results must not depend on it -- same code/config; determinism was never claimed across machines). If the pilot shows both runs progressing at >= the unpacked rate, the remaining pending r2d seeds get converted; 2 runs/GPU under the 10-GPU QOS = ~2x throughput.

- NOTE 08-28 11:10: **RLPD CRITIC DIVERGENCE (E9).** User: "I'm pretty concerned that RLPD has such a low ceiling here, that seems like it must indicate some kind of problem." Confirmed from the SB3 logger tables in every rlpd_*.out: 69/74 runs have critic_loss > 1 at some point (61 > 100; e.g. dH s11 sparse: 0.02 -> 0.34 -> 2.0 -> 551 -> 9880 while ep_rew_mean had reached 0.45; dDP s24 sparse reached ep_rew_mean 0.97 at 75k then critic_loss 0.66 and 0.30 by 98k). Peak TRAINING success median 0.19, >= 0.8 in only 2 runs; final-checkpoint hold < selected hold in 47/74. RLPD's published numbers are therefore "best checkpoint before the critic blew up", not a converged policy. Not entropy (ent_coef decays to ~4e-4 by 30k). LayerNorm present (shared per hidden layer). Prime suspect: gamma 0.998 (500-decision horizon on 100-300-decision episodes; RLPD paper 0.99) + UTD 10. Random-policy exploration stats do NOT differ between worlds (tip 1/20 vs 3/20, touch 5/20 both), so "corrected world is harder" is unsupported.

- NOTE 08-29 04:30: **WM scout report** (`paper/WM_CANDIDATES_2026-08-29.md`, subagent, web+GitHub only). Headline: the official DreamerV3 (arXiv v2 / Nature 2025, danijar/dreamerv3) trains the critic on REPLAY trajectories too (beta_repval 0.3) + EMA critic regulariser + zero-init heads; NM512/dreamerv3-torch predates those revisions. → r2dreamer's `repval 0.3` is a PUBLISHED component; only its return clamp is non-standard. **E8 corrected**: the r2dreamer standard arm = clamp OFF, repval ON. No published method fits all constraints; DEMO3/MoDem need actor BC (collides with the 08-11 no-actor-BC rule); cheapest rule-compatible path = official JAX DreamerV3 with demos in replay (~2-3 person-days). dv3 std arm stays the validity candidate for the torch port.
